// The lobby overlay (overlay 4, lobby.bin): names from every part of the
// overlay, in groups by subject. Three more symbol files hold the names of
// the browser, the online code and the village's second half, by address.
// Format: name = 0xADDRESS; // attributes

// The first function, after the overlay's 64 bytes of padding.
